Options for the game-designer: accept the upper route as the expert line (the chain and the run-speed bounce are the readable ways), or ask for a 4th row of headroom (L3–L6 on row 11, 4 up; the bg bowls then show as pendant lamps, art follows) or 3-tile gaps (the art moves the chains). **The arena entry**: the solver jumps over the step and the Tortenvitrine at 744 (window 25), lands on the arena floor at 805, the lock is at 824 (her centre at x 2061), and she takes the counter during the intro; hits 1283 / 1790 / 2251 from the counter.
  - *The Normal-form boss win* (`cafe_zauner_boss_win_normal`, handwritten: the solver's route to the queue's head, then the grey box's `m7_boss_win_normal` 100 columns on, lock 958, hits 1218 / 1752 / 2230, complete 2590, no hurt). Each required press shifted alone (D-100, ±40, judged on complete + no death + no hurt + three hits): the tap onto the step 28, the jump onto the Tortenvitrine 35, onto the refuge after the lock ≥ 81, the step-offs onto him 29 / 26 / 46 (with the braking 33 / 28 / 41, with the hop back 57 / 46 / 46), the hops back onto the refuge in the stun 29 / 29 / 66, the jump onto the counter 53: **all ≥ 8**.
  - *Route*: with the level shipped, the Bad Ischl → Café Zauner map waits and leads into Stage 9 (D-108). The expected re-cuts fail until the separate task re-cuts them: replays `bad_ischl_tour`, `flow_m6_bad_ischl_teaser_title`, `qa_m6_accept_full_route`, `qa_m6_flow_big_carry_to_teaser` (they expect the title after the teaser) and goldens `bad_ischl_tour_teaser`, `flow_m6_bad_ischl_teaser_title_map_walk`. `flow_m6_hackl_bad_ischl_teaser` still passes (it ends before the teaser) but is on D-108's rename list.

## D-105 Kellner and Wartende: the waiters' trays and the queue (game-designer, M7 planning)
- **Kellner** (`kellner`, 16×32 / 12×26; the hitbox top is his tray): walks **1.0 px/frame** towards the player at activation, turns at walls, enemies on foot and ledges (the Nordic-Walker rule), never stops. **Not stompable**, so the generic contact rule applies (D-070): from above a **bounce without damage or points** off the tray (a normal stomp bounce, so JUMP held lifts her ~3 tiles: the tray is a bounce pad), any other contact hurts. New: the popup **„Komm' gleich!"** (`popup.komm_gleich`) on every tray bounce, stacked like „Bravo!". A bumped block knocks him off (100). Axe **Bounce** (the tray). `sinks_in_swim_water` true, on foot.
- **Wartender** (`wartender`, 16×24 / 12×20, three looks): **a Selfie-Tourist with `still = true`** (D-026's machine: never walks; the selfie schedule and flash; stomp 100 in the chain; bump knock-off; axe Defeat 100). Two differences, both outside core: the level entity name `wartender` is legal in real levels (`still = true` on a `selfie_tourist` stays test-only, the lint unchanged), and the renderer draws the `wartender` sprite **facing right** whatever its `dir`, with the look chosen by spawn column (col mod 3). In core it is a still Selfie-Tourist (no new `EnemyKind`, no new behaviour); whether the parser maps the name onto the `selfie_tourist` spawn with `still` and a look flag, or adds a spawn kind, is the engine's call (record it).
- **Level rules**: a Kellner is penned by walls ≥ 2 high, with ≥ 6 free tiles where he is first met and a refuge ≥ 3 tiles up in his pen; he never paces beside or under the queue. Wartende stand 3 columns apart on the floor (≥ 2 free tiles between heads), on `marmor_punkt` dots, with an upper route over them; no other enemy in the queue's columns.
- **Reasons**: the journey table promised "waiters with trays", and the tray is the boss's whole rule (tray up: bounce; tray down: stomp). The Kellner teaches it with nothing at stake, and the bounce gives the tray a use (the chandeliers). The Kellner is the Reisebus contact on a Nordic-Walker body, so the only new code is a speed and a popup. The queue is the stage's joke made playable. A row of still people is a row of hazards to hop, and with the Abstand dots the spacing allows the classic SMB stomp chain: nine heads give 100 … 8000 and then a life. Jumping the queue is rewarded, and the Oberkellner is the consequence. Reusing the still tourist keeps the queue at zero new behaviour.
- **Fallbacks** (engine's call, record it): Kellner without the popup; Wartende as `selfie_tourist` with `still = true` and the lint allowing it in `cafe_zauner` (they then face the player and use the Selfie-Tourist sprite).
- **Not taken**: a tray you can ride (a moving enemy-as-platform: backlog, D-104); stompable waiters (the tray rule would mean nothing before the boss); a queue that pushes Resi back (a new knockback rule for one joke); a queue of solid people you walk over (a plain wall; and walking on heads without a stomp reads as a bug); a queue that moves forward (backlog, the boss-form idea).
- **As built (E45): no fallback taken.** `EnemyKind::Kellner` (hash id 23, 24 reserved; `Popup::KommGleich` sub-tag 12); tuning `enemies.kellner_hitbox` 12×26, `kellner_speed` 256 (1.0), `kellner_bounce_pose` 8 (validated in `Tuning::parse`: hitbox ≤ 16×32, speed > 0 and < 12 px/frame, pose ≥ 1). Engine calls the spec left open, decided conservatively: (1) **Wartender = a spawn kind, not an enemy kind**: `SpawnKind::Wartender` spawns `Enemy(SelfieTourist)` with `dir = 0` (exactly the `still` machine), so core has no new kind and the M1 tourist's tests cover him; the renderer tells him apart by his spawn's kind (`entities::wartender_look`, look = spawn column mod 3) and never mirrors him. Without the `wartender` sprite he is the Selfie-Tourist's **grey box**, not its sprite (the milestone item; only the D-105 fallback would draw the tourist sprite). (2) **„Komm' gleich!" placement**: centred on the Kellner, `POPUP_STACK_PX` (11) above his tray, where „Bravo!" stands over a score popup (a tray bounce has no score popup); a new popup on every bounce. (3) **The `bounce` anim needs core state**: a bounce sets `state = TRAY_BOUNCE`, `count` = frames since it, back to `WALK` after `kellner_bounce_pose` frames (the bounce frame counts as the first); it changes nothing but the pose (hashed like every enemy state, so the solver sees it; a unit test shows the walk is unchanged). (4) **Points**: the entities table's "100 (bump or axe only)" conflicts with D-105's axe **Bounce**; the decision wins: an axe bounces off his tray for nothing, only a bumped block knocks him off (100, outside the chain). `knock_off_block` takes stompable kinds **and** the Kellner (the Reisebus, Fiaker and cars stay immune). (5) A tray bounce emits `Event::Stomped` like every roof bounce (replays that must show "no stomp" check `score=0` instead). **Level note** (grey box `00_m7_kellner`): stepping off a 2-high wall onto his tray with JUMP held is a coyote jump, not a held bounce (the coyote's 6 frames outlast the 6 px drop); the held bounce comes from a one-way refuge with DOWN + JUMP (dropping through gives no coyote) or from falling out of a jump with JUMP still held; a 4-tile chandelier 2 tiles above a wall top is reachable by a jump from the wall, so the grey box puts it mid-pen. Replays `m7_kellner_tray_bounce`, `_tray_to_luster`, `_side_hit`, `_queue_chain` (5 Wartende: 100 … 1000 = 2500), `_queue_walk`, and its solver replay.

## D-106 The Oberkellner: the boss, his arena and his bills (game-designer, M7 planning)
- **Boss: the Oberkellner, not the queue.** A boss needs one body to read, a telegraph and a weak moment. A queue has none of these: it is many bodies, and its weak point would be the person at the front, which turns the satire against tourists. The head waiter is the queue's guardian, a beloved stock figure of the Austrian coffee house, and his tray gives a rule players already know from the Kellner. The queue stays the stage's set piece (D-105) and the reason for the fight („Hinten anstellen!").
- **The arena is the level's last 20 columns, locked by the side camera itself**: no new camera mode. Since D-031 the side camera clamps at the level's right edge and never scrolls back, and `player::Bounds` keeps her inside its left edge. So once the camera reaches its maximum x, the 320 px screen is a closed room. With `camera_top = 92` (D-104) nothing moves vertically. **Lock** = the first frame of play with the camera at its maximum x and the boss active. From then on the boss's own counter (frames of play; a respawn resets the level and so the fight) drives everything, so the fight is deterministic and the same for every approach. Not taken: locking on the stage clock (an approach-dependent phase at the lock); a fight timer from activation (he activates off screen).
- **The machine** (numbers in game-design.md "The Oberkellner"): `WAIT` → `INTRO` 90 (popup „Hinten anstellen!") → rounds by hits taken. Each round: **Telegraph** (40/36/32: tray high, facing the far wall) → **Dash** to the far wall (2.0/2.5/3.0 px/frame, stopped exactly at the wall face, so dashes alternate sides) → **Verschnaufen** (72/60/48: tray lowered, the hitbox 16×30, stompable) → in rounds 2–3, if not hit, **Volley** (3 or 4 bills at fixed arena columns, a 30-frame hang, then falling at 1.0 with a ±12 px sway; the phase ends when the last bill is gone). **Hit** (stomp or axe in the breather): 1000 + „Unerhört!", **Stun** 60 (no contact). The **third** hit: 5000 + „Bitte sehr!", bills removed, `DONE` (bowing, harmless), and the `goal_stollen` arms (D-107). Contact: no contact in Wait/Intro/Stun/Done; tray up (Telegraph/Dash/Volley): from above a bounce without damage or points, else hurt; breather: from above a hit, else hurt. **Axe**: tray up **Bounce**, breather **a hit** (same as a stomp, one per breather since the Stun passes everything), otherwise **Pass**. Boss points are fixed and outside the stomp chain. `sinks_in_swim_water` true.
- **Fairness rules** (lint + replays): the arena has a 3-high solid refuge at each end and a flat solid floor between them, with nothing solid or one-way above the floor (bills reach the floor, nothing reads as a ledge) and no other enemies or boxes; bills fall only over the floor, ≥ 4 columns apart, never over a refuge; every attack has a telegraph ≥ 30 frames; a dash and a volley never overlap; every breather is at a wall under a refuge's edge. Beatable in all three forms: Normal without a hurt, Zuckerschock, Hackl (the axe changes nothing about the number of breathers needed). The checkpoint is right before the arena (112) with an `M`-box at 117.
- **Testability**: handwritten replays in a grey box `00_m7_boss` and in the real level, one win per form, a tray bounce, a volley dodge, a death with the reset. Each required press of the Normal win is shifted alone (the D-100 method) and must have a window ≥ 8. **Solver**: in a level with an `oberkellner`, `solver::search_progress` adds the boss's hits × a weight larger than the level's width in subpx (the climb pattern of x + height, D-059), and the boss's state, counter, hits and bills are in `Game::world_hash`. **Fallback** (engine's call, record it): if the solver can't win the fight inside its frame budget, its target in such a level is the arena lock (a solver-only end condition), its replay ends there, and the fight is proven by the handwritten replays and their hand-measured windows.
- **Reasons**: the fight uses only reads the game has taught: a Fiaker's telegraph and charge, a refuge above a charger (Stage 1's fountain, Stage 5's shelters), a tray you bounce off (the Kellner), a hazard falling at fixed columns (the Steinschlag), a state that decides whether a stomp works (the officer's salute). The new part is the boss as one machine with hit points and rounds that get faster. Three hits with a stun between them is the SMB3 boss rhythm, and one hit per breather keeps the Hackl from trivialising the fight. The bills are the joke („Zahlen, bitte!") and the second read, and they never overlap a dash, so a Normal Resi on the floor has one thing to read at a time.
- **Fallbacks**: (1) no volley: the rounds are telegraph, dash and breather only, with the same numbers (the fight stays complete and fair). (2) If the boss machine slips the milestone: the `goal_stollen` armed from the start and the Oberkellner as a Fiaker-style charger between the refuges (not stompable), so the stage still ends at the counter. Record which one.
- **Not taken**: a boss that throws plates or cakes (wasting food is no joke at Zauner; the bill is the waiter's own weapon); bills aimed at her (an aimed projectile isn't readable at a fixed position, and fixed columns keep the solver and the replays deterministic); a boss on the stage clock (the lock frame would depend on the approach); hit points shown as a bar (the stun, his askew bow tie and the faster rounds show them).
- **Hash tags**: `EnemyKind::Oberkellner` 25 (25–26 reserved), `EntityKind::Rechnung` 30 (30–31), `Popup::Anstellen` 13, `Popup::Unerhoert` 14, `Popup::BitteSehr` 15 (13–16 reserved). D-105: `EnemyKind::Kellner` 23 (23–24), `Popup::KommGleich` 12. D-107: `Popup::Stollen` 17.
- **As built (engine, M7 task E46): no fallback** (the volley ships; no charger). Level syntax `{ entity = "oberkellner" }` (no fields). Tuning `[enemies]` as specified: `oberkellner_hitbox` [16, 36], `oberkellner_breathe_hitbox` [16, 30], `oberkellner_intro` 90, `oberkellner_telegraph` [40, 36, 32], `oberkellner_dash` [512, 640, 768], `oberkellner_breathe` [72, 60, 48], `oberkellner_stun` 60, `oberkellner_bill_hitbox` [8, 8], `oberkellner_bill_hang` 30, `oberkellner_bill_fall` 256, `oberkellner_bill_sway` 12, `oberkellner_bill_sway_period` 64, plus the points `oberkellner_hit_points` 1000 and `oberkellner_final_points` 5000 (in `[enemies]` beside the boss, as `stein_points`; E47 adds `score.stollen`). `Tuning::parse` checks: the breather box as wide as the tray box and not taller (so a breather at a wall stays flush with its face), every telegraph and the bills' hang ≥ `tuning::MIN_BOSS_TELEGRAPH` (30, the fairness rule), speeds above 0 and below a tile per frame, a bill within its 12×12 sprite, a sway ≤ 16 px. Arena geometry is code, not tuning, because the lint checks it: `enemies::ARENA_COLS` 20, `BILL_ROW` 8, `BILL_COLUMNS` [4, 9, 14] / [3, 7, 11, 15], `ARENA_CLEAR_FROM_ROW` 7.
  - **The lock and the counter**: the lock is the first update that starts with the camera at its right clamp (the camera follows after the entities, so it is the frame after the camera arrives); he is active a screen earlier. `count` is frames into the current state (1 on its first frame); every state is entered from the lock in a fixed chain, so the fight is the same for every approach, and only frames of play advance it (entities don't update while frozen, paused or dying). Hits are a new `Entity::hits` field (0 for every other entity; hashed in `state_hash`, and in `world_hash`'s enemy word, so the solver tells rounds apart). The testbed's `FINISH_HASH` was re-pinned for the new tuning keys and the hashed field (route and frame unchanged).
  - **Dash and breather**: the far wall is the side with more free floor columns ahead of him (a tie faces left); the dash moves with tile collision and the frame his side touches the wall face starts the breather there (flush: x 1952 / 2176 in Stage 9). Open risk 4 (the frame his box changes size): the box changes height with the feet and centre kept, and `prev_y` moves with the top, so the stomp rule measures the middle of the box she touches at its previous position (test `the_frame_his_hitbox_changes_size_is_judged_on_the_new_box`: a fall onto the breather's first frame with the feet 12 px below the new top is a hit, not a side hurt). Done he keeps the tray-up box, stands still and is harmless.
  - **Contact and axes**: `Entity::harmful` is false in Wait/Intro/Stun/Done (no contact, axes pass); otherwise `enemies::oberkellner_contact` (breather from above: a hit; tray up from above: the Fiaker's bounce, feet on the tray, no popup, no points; any other contact and every contact while swimming: hurt). A new axe class `AxeHit::Hit` (the breather; the kind's default is `Bounce`). Hits run through `Game::hit_oberkellner` for stomp and axe alike; the stomp bounces her (JUMP held: the high bounce), the chain neither advances nor resets. He counts as on foot in every state (no walker shares his arena).
  - **Bills**: a bill's hitbox is centred on its column with its top on row 8's top, so it falls 104 px to a floor on row 15 and is gone on the frame it would enter it (hang 30 + 105 ≤ 140, the spec's bound). The game spawns the round's bills on the volley's first frame; the volley ends on the first frame that starts without a bill. A falling bill hurts on any contact and is removed when it does; while she blinks it passes her (it hurts nobody then). Removed also on one-way tiles, in water and hazards, and at the defeat.
  - **Replay expectation** `boss_hits=N` (0..=3; fails in a level without an Oberkellner), so a replay can show the reset after a death.
  - **Grey box** `content/levels/00_m7_boss.level` (40 × 17, `camera_top = 92`, the last 28 columns of Stage 9 with col − 100, plus an `H`-box at (7, row 12) for the Hackl replay) with `m7_boss_win_normal` (left refuge only: hits at 579, 1113, 1591, complete 1951), `_win_big` (hurt by the first dash at the wall, then a jump up out of him onto his head in the breather while blinking), `_win_hackl` (stays on the floor, jumps every dash, one axe per breather, an axe off the tray at the first telegraph and an axe through the stun; every breather is hit, so no volley comes), `_tray_bounce`, `_volley_dodge` (in the lane between the round-2 bills of arena columns 4 and 9), `_death_reset` (a bill kills a Normal Resi; respawn at the checkpoint, `boss_hits=0`), `_solver`. The goal is E47's `goal_stollen` on the counter (the branch merged E47 before this commit), so the wins end with its 10 000.
  - **Solver: the primary plan, no fallback.** `solver::search_progress` adds the Oberkellner's hits × (the level's width + 1 subpixel) in a level with one (the climb pattern, D-059), so a node with one more hit outranks every x; the frame budget stays 3× the width. The grey box is solved with the fight in 2.9 s: complete at frame 1650 (lock, stomps from the counter and the refuge, the bell), 11 jumps, narrowest window 25. E49 keeps it for `00_m7_cafe` and Stage 9 (or takes the D-106 fallback there if the long stage needs it).
  - **Game-level tests from E47's stub note**: an unarmed bell raises no `LevelComplete` (`the_bell_is_armed_only_once_he_is_beaten`), and an unarmed bell does not suppress a hurt in the same frame (`an_unarmed_bell_does_not_suppress_a_hurt`).
  - **Measured windows of the Normal win** (each press, or press group, shifted alone with every other input at its frame, D-100; judged on the replay's outcome: complete, no death, no hurt, three hits; ±40 frames): the jump onto the refuge after the lock 106 (route-limited), the step-offs onto him 29 / 26 / 46 (the step-off with its braking and hop as one group: 57 / 46 / 41), the hops back onto the refuge in the stun 29 / 29 / 85, the jump onto the counter 64; the approach (the tap over the step under the `M`-box 27, onto the refuge 35). All ≥ 8; `normal_win_presses_have_windows_of_at_least_8` checks ±8 on every `check`. The forgiving pattern is a step off the refuge (the bounce is low), a landing beside the stunned waiter and a hop back onto the refuge within the stun; a high bounce straight back needs JUMP pressed in the 3 frames between the end of the coyote time and the stomp, so it is not required.
  - **Finding for the level (E48/level task)**: the `M`-box at (117, row 11) directly left of the 1-high step at 118 leaves a Big or Hackl Resi (30 px) a 2 px band to pass from the floor onto the step under the box; in the grey box she takes the Mozartkugel on the step (`_win_big`) or clears the box with a running jump onto the refuge (`_win_hackl`). The stage should keep a way past it for a big Resi (e.g. the box one column further left, or the step one further right).
- **As built (E49): the Mozartkugel at the step.** A power-up rolls on at 1.0 while it falls off a box, so from an `M`-box directly left of the step (Stage 9: box 117, step 118) it lands **on** the step and turns at the arena's 3-high left refuge (x 1920 in Stage 9), not at the step; it never reaches the arena floor either way (pinned in `tests/m7_cafe.rs` on that layout). To make it turn at the step itself (the game-design text) the box must stand ≥ 3 columns left of the step; the grey box `00_m7_cafe` uses 4 (box 62, step 66), which also leaves a big Resi room under the box (the finding above). The level-designer picks; both satisfy "it never reaches the arena".

## D-107 The finale, the ending and the title's „durchgespielt" mark (game-designer, M7 planning)
- **Stage 9 has no Maibaum** (an exception to "each stage ends at a Maibaum"). Its goal is the **`goal_stollen`** on the counter top: a goal kind like `goal_maibaum`, **armed** once no undefeated Oberkellner is in the level (a level without one: armed from the start, for test levels). The trigger is its 16×16 cell. Unarmed it is drawn closed under its glass bell and touching it does nothing. Reason: the journey's goal is a slice of cake, not a pole. A goal Resi must still walk and jump to after the boss (the SMB axe) keeps the boss and the goal separate, so E46 and E47 build them in parallel, joined only by "armed".
- **The finale goal sequence** (`goal_stollen`): `LevelComplete`, enemies freeze, the timer stops; 10 000 points + „Zaunerstollen!"; **Serve** 90 frames (after she lands): she holds the plate up (`stollen_teller` drawn above her `idle` pose; no new player anim), the Oberkellner bows; then the unchanged **Tally** and **Clear** (`stage.clear`, `stage.cafe_zauner.next` „Mahlzeit, Resi!", the time bonus). The Maibaum's Slide, Hop and Walk don't apply.
- **After Clear**: in the real flow (`Start::Title`) on the route's last station → **`Mode::Ending`**; in every other case → the M1 `ToBeContinued` → title (so `Start::Playing` replays, the solver and lone levels end as before).
- **The ending**: phases **Map** (240: the whole route walked, every station done, Resi on the Café Zauner dot, panel „Ziel erreicht!" / „Salzburg – Café Zauner" / „Zu Fuß. Ohne Bus 150.") → **Scene** (360: the framed `ending_cafe` illustration and three lines) → **Bill** (until START, ≤ 600: the run's tally as a café bill: Punkte, Oblaten gesammelt, Leben übrig, „Trinkgeld: inklusive", „Beehren S' uns wieder!") → **Credits** (6 pages × 180) → **Ende** (until START, ≤ 600) → the **title**. START (a new press, ignored for the first 30 frames of a phase; 60 in Bill and Ende) skips to the next phase or page. Everything has fixed frame counts, so `start = title` replays can run through the ending.
- **The run's Oblaten total** (new, hashed): every Oblate collected since the run started or the last continue; it never wraps (the HUD counter wraps at 100) and resets with the score on a continue. Reason: the bill should show what she collected over the whole journey, and the HUD counter can't. No other new run statistic.
- **The credits** name no living person: they credit the roles (the game was made by AI agents) and the cast in operetta-playbill style („Es spielten:", „Weiters:"), and thank Café Zauner (since 1832), the Lokalbahn (until 1957), Postbus 150 („trotzdem") and everyone who queues. The last page: „Bei der Herstellung / dieses Spiels wurde / keine Oblate / verschwendet." (Nickerl's broken Oblaten, 1905).
- **The „durchgespielt" mark**: after Ende, the title resets the run as always and shows a Zaunerstollen at Resi's feet, „Durchgespielt! Mahlzeit!" and the prompt „Nochmal? Drück START". It is a `Game` field (hashed, survives `end_run`, set only by the ending), session-only: no save games (D-042), a reload forgets it. START starts a new run from Salzburg exactly as before. Reason: the player should see that the journey is complete, at no cost to determinism or the no-saves rule. Not taken: a stage select after the first clear (backlog, D-042's revisit note); saving the mark (MW, needs storage on the web).
- **Replays**: new expectations `state=ending` (any phase) and `cleared=true|false` (the title mark). The renderer draws everything from `Game` state (the phase, its frame, the run totals); nothing in the ending is random or clock-based.
- **Fallbacks**: Map phase skipped (straight to Scene); the Scene as text only on navy if `ending_cafe` is missing; the Bill as a D-037 panel without `ending_zettel`; credits as text only. The minimum is Scene (text), Bill, Credits (text), Ende, and the title mark.
- **Not taken**: a cinematic of the Oberkellner walking to her table (Serve keeps her where she landed: a scripted walk from any refuge or floor position needs pathing); a final score multiplier; a „Game Over" for the timer during the ending (the ending has no timer).
- **As built (E47)**: (1) *Last station*: the ending follows the Clear in the real flow (`Start::Title`, a route) when the cleared stage is the journey's last station (`STATIONS[8]`, Café Zauner), whatever its goal kind; a `goal_stollen` level elsewhere on a route goes on to the route map (or its teaser) as any stage does, and `Start::Playing` games and lone levels end in „Fortsetzung folgt …". Test routes reach the ending by putting the level on station 9 (`flow_m7_finale_*`: `route = salzburg … bad_ischl m7_finale`). (2) *Serve*: on the touch she loses her horizontal speed and keeps her vertical speed (a touch while rising rises on, then falls); the fall uses the player's gravity and stops on solid and one-way tiles (and at the level's bottom edge); the 90 frames count from the frame she stands; input is ignored as in every goal sequence. The camera follows her y as in Slide/Hop/Walk (so `camera_top` holds). (3) *Plate*: `stollen_teller` stays above her head through the Tally and the Clear panel of a `goal_stollen` (the renderer finds the touched goal by the sequence's `pole_x`/`base_y`) too (she stands there; a plate vanishing when the panel appears would read as a glitch), drawn 1 px above her drawn look (16×24 / 16×32), not the hitbox; the „Zaunerstollen!" and score popups rise over it in the Serve's first 30 frames. (4) *Bell state*: `zu` while unarmed, `leer` from the touch on (the whole goal sequence), else `offen`. (5) *START timing*: as for the game over's prompt, a press counts once the phase (or credits page) is `start_ignore_frames` (30) frames old (60 in Bill and Ende): a phase entered at frame F skips at F + 30 at the earliest; each credits page has its own 30-frame delay. (6) *Bill layout*: the pixel-artist's `ending_zettel` layout at (72, 18): lines at y 18 + 16 + 12k, title k0 and thanks k8 centred in `R` without shadow, labels k2–k4 and the tip k6 at x 80 in `d`, numbers right-aligned to x 239 in `Y` with a `k` outline; without the sprite the same 176×144 box is a D-037 panel with `w` text and the `k` shadow. Lines after the title appear at frames 20, 40, …, 100. (7) *Credits*: the present lines of a page are centred as a block (pitch 12, 6 px more below a title, title `Y`, lines `w`); page 2's optional cast sprites are not drawn (fallback). (8) *Ende*: „Ende" in `Y` at y 70, the two lines at 92 and 104 in `w`, no prompt. (9) *Marked title*: `title.cleared` in `Y` with a full `k` outline at y 100, the plate 16 px right of Resi's centre on her ground line. (10) *Hashes*: `oblaten_total`, the ending state, `cleared` and the new tuning keys (`score.stollen`, `goal.serve_frames`, `[ending]`) are hashed, which re-pinned the testbed's `FINISH_HASH`; no replay pins a hash, every existing replay and golden is unchanged. (11) The Oblaten total also resets on the title's START (it is part of `reset_run`, like the score).

## D-108 The route after M7: Café Zauner is playable, the teaser leaves the real route, M6 re-cuts (game-designer, M7 planning)
- Shipping `09_cafe_zauner.level` (+ its `options::LEVELS` line) makes the last station playable (D-047). The map after Bad Ischl ends in **Wait** with the prompt „Weiter mit START", then the Café Zauner intro, and the real route has **no teaser any more**. The labels don't change: on that screen Resi stands on the Café Zauner dot as she did in the teaser, so D-098's slots hold (Café Zauner's primary slot ends at x 290; Bad Ischl's at row 92). The Teaser phase and `ui.to_be_continued` stay in the engine for test routes whose next level is missing (`flow_route_teaser`) and for `Start::Playing` and lone levels (the M1 ending).
- **Re-cuts** (as D-053/D-066/D-074/D-092 did): every M6 replay and golden whose ending changes because the Bad Ischl → Café Zauner map now waits and leads into Stage 9 is re-cut with an art-director verdict. `bad_ischl_tour` ends on the route map in Wait (golden `bad_ischl_tour_teaser` → `bad_ischl_tour_route_map`). `flow_m6_bad_ischl_teaser_title` → `flow_m6_bad_ischl_to_cafe_zauner` and `flow_m6_hackl_bad_ischl_teaser` → `flow_m6_hackl_bad_ischl_to_cafe_zauner` end on the Café Zauner intro with the run carried over (`stage=cafe_zauner`, `state=stage_intro`, `power=hackl` for the second); `qa_m6_flow_big_carry_to_teaser` and `qa_m6_accept_full_route` end on the Café Zauner intro too. The new full route is `qa_m7_accept_full_route` (title → ending → title with the mark). The map-walk golden keeps its frame (the walk is unchanged; only the panel's third line can differ). Orphaned teaser goldens are deleted. Every other M6 golden stays bit-identical (the Bad Ischl level doesn't change).
- **Route-map goldens**: Bad Ischl → Café Zauner in Wait, Normal and Hackl form (replacing the two teaser shots), and the ending's Map phase (every station done).
- **`--level cafe_zauner`** starts the run at Stage 9 with the default carry-over; clearing it runs the ending (it is the last station).

## D-109 M7 integration: the Stage 9 kinds together (engine-engineer, E49)
- **Context**: E44–E48 built the Stage 9 kinds in parallel worktrees (the E46 merge `72b9c5f` resolved the E45/E47 conflicts in `Game::interact`, `SpawnKind::ALL`, `screens::TEXT_KEYS`, the hash tags and `FINISH_HASH`). E49 checks them together in the grey box `00_m7_cafe` and `crates/jnr-core/src/tests/m7_cafe.rs`. No `// E46:` stub was left (E46 had replaced it). No simulation code changed, so every replay, golden, `FINISH_HASH` and Stage 1–8 solver route is bit-identical.
- **Grey box `00_m7_cafe`** (88 × 17, side camera, `camera_top = 92`, `bg_zauner_salon` at 16, `min_jump_window = 5`): every M7 kind, tile and prop: a Kellner pen (2-high vitrine at 10–11, the hat shelf as a 3-up refuge) whose right end is the first of five Wartende on `marmor_punkt` dots (here on purpose against D-105's level rule, to pin the turn), chandeliers over the queue, a Kurgast between a 2-high and a 3-high vitrine, the checkpoint (59), an `M`-box at (62, row 11) 4 columns before a 1-high step, and the arena (68–87) with the Oberkellner at 82 and the `goal_stollen` on the counter. **Solver: D-106's primary plan holds, no fallback**: complete at frame 1933 with the fight (three hits), 20 jumps, narrowest window 13 (frame 41, x 101: the jump onto the first vitrine), 43 280 search deaths, 3.6 s; replay `m7_cafe_solver`.
- **How the kinds meet** (each pinned by a test): a Kellner turns at a Wartender (on foot, `dir = 0`, never moves) and at a lying Kurgast (his chair widened by the berth, D-102); the Mozartkugel turns before the arena (D-106 as-built note on the box column); the bell arms on exactly the frame of the third hit (not on hits 1–2) and a respawn never arms it (with two hits taken, or after the defeat: the level reset brings him back unbeaten, so `boss_undefeated` holds again); a death in a volley respawns her at the checkpoint with the boss waiting at his spawn, no hits, no bills, the `M`-box whole and the clock at 0, and the next lock rebuilds the same fight frame by frame as a first lock; a thrown Hackl passes through the stunned boss (no hit, no bounce, it sticks in the wall behind him); the Kellner, a Wartender and the Oberkellner are lost in swim water in play.
- **The fixed room camera**: in the grey box on the last station of a test route (`Start::Title`) the camera stays at y 92 on every frame of the title, the intro, a pause, the whole solver route with the lock and the fight, the goal sequence, every ending phase and the marked title, and through a death in the arena, the respawn intro, a game over and the continue. For the real stage: the xtask test `fixed_room_cameras_never_move_vertically_in_any_replay` checks every replay of a level whose `camera_top` is its bottom clamp (today the `m7_boss_*` and `m7_cafe` replays; Stage 9's replays join it automatically).
- **Merge review (E45/E46/E47 interplay)**: the code-reviewer audited `72b9c5f`/`12f70eb` with E49 and APPROVED it with nothing blocking: in `Game::interact` the boss's stomp arm comes before the Kurgast and generic stomp arms (his points stay outside the chain), `tray_bounce` returns nothing for anything but a Kellner (no „Komm' gleich!" on the boss's tray), the bill arm hurts only while falling and every hurt still yields to an armed goal; `goal_spawn` and `level_parts` order the goals alike; `SpawnKind::ALL`/`name`/`from_name` agree (38); the hash tags are distinct (Kellner 23, Oberkellner 25, Rechnung 30, popups 12–15 and 17, Serve 6) and `hits` takes its own bits in `world_hash`; `TEXT_KEYS` lists every text popup now.
- **Kept dependency (recorded, no change)**: `boss_undefeated` counts a **removed** Oberkellner as beaten, so a boss lost in swim water would arm the bell (the test shows a boss in a swim cell is removed). Only the arena lint (no water, nothing but floor in the arena) and the side camera (he never despawns behind it) prevent that; a future level kind that can remove him must keep the bell closed explicitly.
- **Small fixes**: `marmor_punkt` → `marmor` autotile alias (D-104 addendum); `popup.hoppala` was the one text popup missing from `screens::TEXT_KEYS` (it existed in de.toml); the popup test now covers every text popup and their TEXT_KEYS membership.

## D-110 M7 review: Stage 9 as built, difficulty, the queue chandeliers, the queue and its signs (game-designer, M7)
- **Accepted, all six deviations of the D-104 addendum "Stage 9 Café Zauner as built"**: (1) the `M`-box at **(115, row 11)** (supersedes the column 117 in D-104 and D-106). The Mozartkugel lands at 117 and turns at the step's face. A big Resi passes from the floor at 116–117 onto the step, which settles E46's finding. After a respawn at 112 the box is 3 columns away. (2) Boxes clear of the back wall's mirrors and window: `M` **12** (supersedes D-104's 14), `?` 17, 52 and 108. (3) The entrance Oblaten on row 12 at 7–11. (4) „k.u.k. Hof- / zuckerbäcker" at 21, beside the coat stand. (5) `kuchentheke` from (136, 14) with `dx` 32 (the E49 rule), and `luster_deko` on the free chain columns. (6) Oblaten 46 + 3 = 49, inside the target. game-design.md "Stage 9" now describes the level as built, plus the changes below.
- **Difficulty: no tightening. The 6–9 target is withdrawn for Stage 9** (supersedes D-104's "design target narrowest 6–9 in sections 1–4"; the gate `min_jump_window = 5` stays). The counted narrowest is 18 (pen B onto the `?`-box at 52; pen C onto Vitrine D). The target was written for jumps, but the café has no pits and no lethal ground. A missed jump there costs nothing, and a counted window can only shrink through an artificial squeeze (a low lintel, a stopper) that has no place in a café and would break D-104's "gentler than Bad Ischl". The risk in sections 1–4 comes from contact, which the metric doesn't measure (D-073, D-100): a Kellner at 1.0 px/frame that can't be stomped, two hazards in pen C, and nine Wartende whose sides hurt. A Normal death in the queue also sends her back to the start, because the checkpoint comes after it. The tight moments are the queue's optional skill lines: the run-speed bounce (release windows 7 / 12 / 13 / 21) and the nine-head chain (≥ 4). The climax is the boss, the only part that asks for timing read under pressure and gets faster round by round. Its required presses (≥ 26 by hand) are generous on purpose (D-106: patience, not precision). Ramp of counted narrowest windows: Strobl 8, Bad Ischl 5, Café Zauner 18 + the boss. Not taken: moving the `?`-box at 52 or Vitrine D to squeeze the solver's take-offs (a human can stop on the floor before either, so nothing would change for her); a pit (D-104: a café floor doesn't kill).
- **The queue chandeliers L3–L6 are 5 wide** (supersedes "3 wide" in the Stage 9 chandelier rule for L3–L6; L1 and L2 stay 3 wide). Exact: `luster` on row 10 at **L3 74–78, L4 81–85, L5 88–92, L6 95–99**, i.e. add `-` at (74, 10), (78, 10), (81, 10), (85, 10), (88, 10), (92, 10), (95, 10), (99, 10). The gaps become 2 tiles (79–80, 86–87, 93–94), and Vitrine D → L3 becomes a 3-tile gap 2 up. The centres don't move (cols 76, 83, 90, 97: chains at x mod 640 = 584, 56, 168, 280), so `bg_zauner_salon` is unchanged, and `tile_luster` autotiles any run (curled ends, middles between). The Oblaten don't move either (row 9 over the band centres, row 8 over the gaps). Reasons: with 4-tile gaps and 3 tiles of air under the ceiling, every hop needed a run and a jump at the edge inside the coyote frames (3–4 frames). The spec offers this route as the bypass of the queue, so it must be the calm way. A big Resi (her head meets the ceiling after ~18 px of rise) hardly gets across 64 px at all. Not taken: (a) row 11. A held stomp bounce off a Wartender lifts her feet to about y 164–172, above row 11's top (176), so the chain over the queue would land on the chandeliers and break. Row 10 (top 160) is out of reach of every head bounce, as the chain replay shows under L3–L6. (b) 3-tile gaps with 3-wide bands, which would move the background's chains (D-104 art exception 2). (c) 4-wide bands, which would leave the chains 8 px off centre.
  - *Conditions*: a handwritten replay **`cafe_zauner_luster_route`**: Normal, walk speed (no run button), Vitrine D → L3 → L4 → L5 → L6 → the floor right of 99, no stomp, no hurt, taking the row-8/9 Oblaten. Each hop's press is shifted alone (D-100) and must have a window **≥ 8**. A Zuckerschock pass of the same route must complete, and its windows are recorded. Re-run the solver and `cafe_zauner_queue_chain`: neither may land on L3–L6. If the chain's first hop off Vitrine D now lands on L3's new column 74, shorten that hop's hold. Fallback if a Normal hop is below 8: the upper route is recorded in game-design as the expert line, and nothing else moves.
- **The queue: spacing 3 stays, and the nine-head chain's 4-frame release is accepted.** That chain is an optional skill line for the 1-up (the D-105 showcase). The gate binds the solver's counted presses only (D-073), and a life for nine clean bounces may ask for precision. The readable ways past the queue, in the order a player finds them: (a) **stomp them one at a time**: a short hop onto the next head, then land where he stood. This replaces the spec's "a walking jump over each": hopping over a still Wartender from a standstill into the next 32 px gap is precision work (1–4 by the shift). A stomp clears his dot, so every step makes room. (b) The run-speed bounce. (c) The chandeliers. The satire is unchanged: Resi is the queue-jumper, and the queue sulks on its dots.
  - *Condition*: `cafe_zauner_queue_walk` is redefined as (a). Nine single stomps from the floor with JUMP tapped, landing on the floor before each next hop, no hurt, all nine squished. Each hop's press is shifted alone and must be **≥ 8** (expected to be route-limited, since she stands before each hop). If any is below 5, the game-designer re-decides (D-104's fallback: spacing 4, the queue moved left).
- **Signs: no Wartender stands in front of a board's text.** „Wartezeit: / ab hier ewig" at 80 stands behind Wartender 79. His drawn sprite (x 1266–1279, from y 218) covers 2–3 letters of both lines („Wa…eit:"). In a queue spaced 48 px with 14 px figures, the clear space between two figures is 34 px. That is less than any two-line text here (41–71 px), so no board fits in 73–97. „Abstand / halten" at 72 ends at x 1172, inside the first Wartender's outline (from 1170). Exact moves (all on row 14):
  - `j` (`sign_wartezeit`) 80 → **61**, in pen C. It now stands beside the Kurgast who brought his own deck chair, and it's in the room of „1 Melange: / 3 Stunden": the wait starts in the salon.
  - `i` (`sign_abstand`) 72 → **68**, on the floor between Vitrines C and D, right before the dots. Its text spans x 1075–1116; the first Wartender's sprite starts at 1170.
  - `h` (`sign_posten`) 61 → **109**, before the checkpoint. „Erst essen, / dann posten" now faces the Tortenvitrine ahead, and the Wartende behind her are the ones with phones.
  - The Oblaten at 107–111 move from row 13 to **row 12** (D-088: on row 13 they'd sit on the board at 109, whose top is at y 212; row 12 ends at 208, as at the entrance).
  - Signs then stand at 4, 11, 21, 31, 37, 45, 52, 61, 68, 101, 109, 115, all ≥ 6 apart (109 → 115 is exactly 6). Why not elsewhere: 64–66 and 69–70 are vitrines. At 71 the second line ends at 1178 behind the first Wartender's body (from 1171). Four boards in 45–68 with ≥ 6 spacing need every salon board moved. The de.toml texts don't change (the sign keys' "order of the stage" comment is cosmetic).
- **Boss: accepted as measured.** Every required press of the Normal win is ≥ 26 (step 28, Tortenvitrine 35, refuge ≥ 81, step-offs 29 / 26 / 46, hops back 29 / 29 / 66, counter 53) against D-106's ≥ 8.
- **`time_limit` 200 stays provisional** until QA's relaxed run with the fight (D-045, ZEIT clock), set once as for Strobl and Bad Ischl.
- **The D-108 re-cut plan is confirmed.** They fail exactly as expected, because the Bad Ischl → Café Zauner map now waits and leads into Stage 9:
  - Replays: `bad_ischl_tour` ends on the route map in Wait. `flow_m6_bad_ischl_teaser_title` → `flow_m6_bad_ischl_to_cafe_zauner`, ending on the Café Zauner intro with the run carried over. `qa_m6_accept_full_route` and `qa_m6_flow_big_carry_to_teaser` end on the Café Zauner intro.
  - Goldens: `bad_ischl_tour_teaser` → `bad_ischl_tour_route_map`. `flow_m6_bad_ischl_teaser_title_map_walk` → `flow_m6_bad_ischl_to_cafe_zauner_map_walk`, same frame.
  - `flow_m6_hackl_bad_ischl_teaser` still passes but is renamed `flow_m6_hackl_bad_ischl_to_cafe_zauner` and extended to the Café Zauner intro (`power=hackl`): the hackl route-map golden in Wait comes from it.
  - New: the route-map goldens (Normal, Hackl, the ending's Map phase) and `qa_m7_accept_full_route`. Teaser goldens left without a replay are deleted. Each new or changed golden gets an art-director verdict.
  - Order: since a commit needs a green `verify`, the re-cuts land on this branch before the level is committed. They don't depend on this review's level changes (no Bad Ischl change, and the flows end on the Stage 9 intro).
- **Art notes, decided**: (a) boards in front of vitrines (21, 31, 37, 52, 68) are accepted. Boards are drawn in the entity pass, in front of tiles, and a board in front of a solid vitrine suggests no ledge (D-038). (b) The 5-wide chandelier ring on one centre chain: the art-director confirms it with one shot of L3–L4. If it reads as unbalanced, the pixel-artist may add a second, shorter pair of chains to the band ends in `bg_zauner_salon` at the same centres; the collision doesn't change. (c) The `M`-boxes over „Sie werden / platziert" (12) and „Reserviert / seit 1832" (115) keep 20 px above the board tops: accepted.
- **Touched**: `09_cafe_zauner.level` (level-designer): the eight `luster` cells, the three sign cells and the five Oblaten above, plus the header's column list. Then `levels lint`, re-solve, record the route and the narrowest window (gate 5), and re-cut `cafe_zauner_solver` and every Stage 9 replay that copies its route (`cafe_zauner_queue_chain`, `cafe_zauner_boss_win_normal`, `cafe_zauner_mozartkugel_step` if they diverge). Add the two replays above, and put the measured windows in an addendum here. Docs: game-design.md "Stage 9" (this review). Stages 1–8 are untouched.
- **D-110 addendum (level-designer, M7): the changes as built, the measures, the D-108 re-cuts.**
  - *Level*: exactly as listed above (`luster` L3 74–78, L4 81–85, L5 88–92, L6 95–99; `j` 61, `i` 68, `h` 109; Oblaten 107–111 on row 12; header column list). `levels lint` clean. Signs at 4, 11, 21, 31, 37, 45, 52, 61, 68, 101, 109, 115.
  - *Solver* (re-solved, `--write`): complete **2278**, 15 jumps, **narrowest counted window 12** (frame 388, x 969: from pen C's floor over Vitrine C onto Vitrine D, −2..9, fail/fail), next 14+ (route-bounded) and 21–25; gate 5 holds. Its queue pass is unchanged in kind: from Vitrine D it bounces over six heads at run speed (stomps 466–625), release windows 13 / 15 / 13 / 25. It never lands on L3–L6. Arena: lock 824, hits 1283 / 1792 / 2250 from the counter, complete 2278.
  - *Re-cut on the new solver prefix* (same stories): `cafe_zauner_queue_chain` (stops on Vitrine D at 483, jump 484, stomps 521 … 734, „+1 Leben" at 766, floor 800; never on L3–L6; windows 8 / 7 / 7 / 6 / 5 / 20 / 21 / 21 / 21), `cafe_zauner_mozartkugel_step` (unchanged frames: bump 748, the turn at the step, PowerUp 844), `cafe_zauner_boss_win_normal` (lock 954, hits 1214 / 1748 / 2226, complete 2586; every window as before: 28 / 35 / ≥ 81 / 29, 26, 46 / 29, 29, 66 / 53).
  - *`cafe_zauner_luster_route`* (Normal, walk speed, no run button; Vitrine D → L3 524 → L4 635 → L5 746 → L6 856 → the floor right of 99 at 972, all 18 row-8/9 Oblaten, no stomp, no hurt). Each hop's press shifted alone (D-100, every other input at its frame; the same result when each hop is cut 40 frames after its landing and judged on standing on the target band): **10** (Vitrine D → L3, −5..4, limited by the walk before it), **13, 13, 12**. All ≥ 8: the upper route is the calm way; no fallback.
  - *`cafe_zauner_luster_route_big`* (Zuckerschock): from the start, the Mozartkugel from the `M`-box at 12, through the pens on the vitrines, L1 and the `?`-box at 52 without a hurt, onto Vitrine D (1285), then the same route at walk speed (L3 1337 … L6 1666, floor 1783), still big, 18 Oblaten, no stomp in the queue. Windows **10, 8, 7, 8**: a big Resi crosses the 2-tile gaps, with one hop at 7.
  - *`cafe_zauner_queue_walk`* (D-110 (a)): off Vitrine D onto the floor, then nine single stomps, each a hop from a standstill with JUMP tapped (1–10 frames, released before the stomp) and a little steering right, landing on the stomped Wartender's dot (stomps 576 … 1088, 100 each, no hurt, all nine squished). Each hop cut after its 50-frame stand: **the press shifted alone 8 / 6 / 7 / 7 / 11 / 6 / 8 / 10 / 37**. The shift fails because the steering after the press is held at its frame, so a later press steers less: it is not a timing limit, since she stands before each hop. **The hop shifted as a whole (press and steering together) 42 / 10 / 23 / 29 / 24 / 23 / 27 / 30 / 50.** No press is below 5, so the spacing stays 3 (D-110). Four presses are 6–7 by the strict measure, short of the expected ≥ 8, for the game-designer to note.
  - *D-108 re-cuts* (inputs unchanged unless noted; each header explains it): `bad_ischl_tour` ends on the route map in Wait (2822, `state=route_map`; shot `teaser` → `route_map`, frame 2640). `flow_m6_bad_ischl_teaser_title` → **`flow_m6_bad_ischl_to_cafe_zauner`** (idle tail 800 → 910: the map in Wait, the Café Zauner intro at 5643, ends 5703 with score 35600, lives 3, 75 Oblaten; shot `map_walk` at 5330 unchanged). `flow_m6_hackl_bad_ischl_teaser` → **`flow_m6_hackl_bad_ischl_to_cafe_zauner`** (route header + `cafe_zauner`, idle tail 563 → 733: the intro at 2016 as Hackl; shot `teaser` → `route_map`, frame 1784). `qa_m6_accept_full_route` (tail 761 → 910: the intro at 32024, Hackl, lives 7, score 154150) and `qa_m6_flow_big_carry_to_teaser` (name kept; tail 760 → 910: the intro at 11746, big, lives 4). Orphaned goldens deleted: `bad_ischl_tour_teaser`, `flow_m6_bad_ischl_teaser_title_map_walk`, `flow_m6_hackl_bad_ischl_teaser_teaser`. **New goldens pending the art-director** (not accepted; actuals in `/workspaces/.cargo-target/m7-review/goldens-lvl9/`): `bad_ischl_tour_route_map`, `flow_m6_bad_ischl_to_cafe_zauner_map_walk`, `flow_m6_hackl_bad_ischl_to_cafe_zauner_route_map`, plus the scene `cafe_zauner_scene_l3_l4_x1120.png` for art note (b). No xtask coupling test names these replays.

## D-111 M7 QA feature pass: the first vitrine, the time limit, the marked title, the title backdrop, the Oberkellner at the wall (game-designer, M7)
- **Bug 1, the `?`-box at (17, row 11) blocks a walking Resi at the first vitrine: the box moves to (8, row 11)** (supersedes the `?` 17 in D-110 (2) / the D-104 addendum's deviation (2); the `M`-box stays at 12). The box stood in the column right before the 2-high vitrine at 18–19. Between its bottom (y 192) and the vitrine's top (y 208) there are 16 px, less than Normal Resi's 22, so any arc that reaches the vitrine top from the left has to go over the box (top y 176). A walking jump peaks with her feet at ~y 181, so only a running jump passes (QA: the solver's route, the walking-only beam search stops at x 276, a big Resi passes only with a running jump started in x ~208 … the box). That breaks the reach rule ("step-ups ≤ 3 with a walking jump") on the stage's first required step-up. Exact change in `09_cafe_zauner.level`, row 11 (grid line 12 of `[map]`): `?` at col 17 → `.`, and `.` at col 8 → `?`. Nothing else moves.
  - *Why col 8*: the box stays off every back-wall frame, so the art-director's rule (no box in front of a mirror unless it keeps 4–8 px off the mirror's bottom bar) is met by not being in front of a mirror at all. `bg_zauner_salon` at factor 16: the first window with its drapes spans x 51–108 (cols 3–6, the sill at screen y 106–107), the pilaster with its sconce x 155–170 (col 10, `luster_deko` hangs there), the first mirror x 215–264 (cols 13–16). Col 8 (x 128–144) is plain cream wall, 20 px clear of the drapes and 11 px of the pilaster. Cols 13–16 are the mirror; cols 9–11 touch the pilaster or crowd the `M`-box; cols 18 and up are the vitrine and the pen (with the Kellner, the hat shelf at 23–25 and its Oblaten on row 10). Row 11 is kept (4 up, the box rule holds; a box on row 12 at 17 would put its top on the mirror bar's height, y 100, and turn the vitrine approach into a 3-up box step).
  - *What it gives*: the first thing ahead of the start (col 2) is a `?`-box over the entrance's Oblaten line (it sits over the Oblate at (8, row 12), the pattern of the box at 108 over 107–111, D-110), then the `M`-box at 12, then 5 free floor columns 13–17 before the vitrine: a plain 2-up step for every form at walk speed. Under both boxes the floor has 48 px of headroom (a big Resi walks under). The Mozartkugel from 12 still rolls right, turns at the vitrine's face and rolls back under the `?`-box to the door. Oblaten 46 + 3 = 49 unchanged.
  - *New reach rule for Stage 9* (game-design.md "Difficulty and reach rules (stage 9)", Reach row): **the 2 columns in front of a required step-up's face are free of solid tiles (boxes included) up to 3 tiles above the step's top**, so a box can't close the walking jump's arc. Checked by hand against the level after the change: Vitrine 18 (16–17 free), Vitrine A 32 (30–31: a board only), Vitrine B 48 (46–47), Vitrine C 64 (62–63: enemies only), Vitrine D 69 (67–68: a board only), the step 118 (116–117), the Tortenvitrine (the step itself), the counter (135–136: the Oberkellner and a back prop). A `levels lint` check for it is welcome (tooling backlog, not required for M7).
  - *Conditions (level-designer)*: change the two cells and the header (section 1: „?-box 8"; the mirror note keeps the `M`-box 12). `levels lint`, re-solve (`--write`; gate 5), and record the route, the narrowest window and Kellner #1's meeting in a D-111 addendum. The solver will now step onto the vitrine directly, so the tray drop near frame 129 may move: record where Kellner #1 is met. Re-cut every Stage 9 replay on the old solver prefix that diverges (`cafe_zauner_solver`, `_queue_chain`, `_boss_win_normal`, `_mozartkugel_step`, `_luster_route_big` and any other that touches col 17 or the box). Bring QA's `qa_m7_walk_jump_blocked_at_first_vitrine` (branch `qa-m7`; held walking jump at frame 181 from x 276, expects `player_x >= 290`) onto the milestone branch: it must pass unchanged. Add **`cafe_zauner_walk_only`**: Normal, no run button, from the start onto Vitrine D's top (col 69) with no hurt (the tray drop optional; waiting for the Kellner allowed). With `cafe_zauner_queue_walk` (from Vitrine D on) it proves the walking reach for sections 1–3. Any required press in it below 8 by the D-100 shift goes back to the game-designer.
- **`time_limit` = 200 is final** (D-045; replaces "provisional" in D-104/D-110). QA's relaxed run `qa_m7_cafe_zauner_relaxed` uses 57.4 s of ZEIT up to the touch, with the fight. 3 × 57.4 = 172.2, rounded up to 180 and clamped to the minimum 200. The box move only makes the first vitrine easier, so it can't lengthen the relaxed run, and no re-measurement is needed. Oblaten 46 + 3 = 49 against the target 50 ± 10: the milestone item is ticked. Ramp: Strobl 230 (relaxed 75.05 s), Bad Ischl 210 (69.67 s), Café Zauner 200 (57.4 s, the clamp: a short stage with a boss, the most generous ratio of the route, fitting a finale).
- **Observation 1, mashing START skips the marked title: fixed with an ignore window.** When the title is entered **from the ending** (after Ende), START is ignored for its first **60 frames** (the Bill and Ende value, D-107 (5)): a press counts once the marked title is 60 frames old. Every other entry to the title (boot, after a game over's title, after „Fortsetzung folgt …") is unchanged: START is accepted at once, as players expect at the start. The mark itself, the prompt („Nochmal? Drück START", blinking from the first frame) and the run reset don't change. Reason: the „Durchgespielt! Mahlzeit!" screen is the journey's last word, and a player mashing through the credits should see it for at least a second. 60 frames are the same pause as Bill and Ende, so the rhythm is familiar. Not taken: a longer or an unskippable mark (the ending already has ~25 s of fixed phases); ignoring START on every title (it would slow the boot, which nobody asked for).
  - *Engine*: a frame count since the title was entered (or an "entered from the ending" flag) gates START; reuse the `[ending]` 60-frame key or add a key (engine's call, record it; validate it in `Tuning::parse`); hashed with the title state. Unit test: on the marked title a START at frames 1–59 does nothing and at 60 starts the run; a title after a game over or at boot still takes START at once. Replays that press START on the marked title within its first 60 frames (check `qa_m7_accept_full_route` and `flow_m7_finale_*`) are re-cut to press at ≥ 60, each header saying why; no golden changes (the drawing is the same).
- **Observation 2, the title over the café backdrop on a run that starts at Stage 9: accepted as a debug-only path, no engine change.** The title draws the run's first stage's backdrop. In the shipped flow that is always Salzburg (the native default and the web build start the route at Salzburg; D-107's run reset returns there), and there the layout is approved. Only `--level cafe_zauner` and test routes whose first station is the café draw the title over the salon, where the subtitle and the mark overlap the box and chandelier art. Forcing Salzburg's backdrop on every title would change the title of every `--level` run and test route (and any golden of one) for a view that players never see. *Condition*: no golden, tour shot or review shot of the title (plain or marked) is taken on a run whose first stage isn't Salzburg; the M7 tour and flow shots of the marked title come from `qa_m7_accept_full_route` or a `flow_m7_finale_*` route starting at Salzburg. If a stage select ever lets players start elsewhere (D-042/D-107 backlog), this is revisited: the title then uses Salzburg's backdrop.
- **Observation 3, the Oberkellner overlaps the left Tortenvitrine by a few pixels at the wall: accepted, no art or engine change.** His hitbox (16 px) stops flush with the wall face by design (D-106: every breather is right under a refuge's edge, so a step off the refuge lands on him, and all hand-measured windows ≥ 26 depend on that geometry). His 32 px sprite is wider than the hitbox, so the bow, the breath, the napkin and the tray reach a few pixels over the vitrine. Entities are drawn in front of tiles, as a board in front of a vitrine is (D-110 art note (a)), and a waiter leaning against his own cake vitrine reads as a waiter, not as a ledge or a hitbox error (his contact is his hitbox; she stands on the vitrine above him). An engine stop offset would move every breather off the refuge's edge and re-open the measured windows for a cosmetic gain. An art trim would cut his best poses (the bow is the fight's punchline). The same holds at the right wall against the counter. The art-director may still flag it in the tour's arena shots; then the pixel-artist pulls only the overhang of the `verschnaufen`/`verbeugen` frames inside 4 px of the hitbox, without moving the anchor.
- **Touched**: this entry; game-design.md "Stage 9" (the time-limit line, the Reach row, section 1's boxes, the solver note, the marked title in "Finale and ending", the Oberkellner's overlap note); milestones.md M7 (the `time_limit`/Oblaten item ticked). To do: `09_cafe_zauner.level` (level-designer: the two cells, the header, re-solve, the re-cuts, `cafe_zauner_walk_only`, QA's replay on the branch); the engine (the marked title's 60-frame START window and its re-cuts). No de.toml change.
